## Mandate

Give v5 v4's four fixes on the mount-index / files surface at `236c04e35`'s
shape, server side only, producing the `FILES` stacking commit. (1) **Bugs
260 / 261** — a chat upload (every one is a blob: `.md`, `.txt`, `.json`
included) becomes readable, greppable and searchable: `extract_blob_text`
lifted out of `store_mount_file` as the ONE place blob text extraction lives,
gated on `is_extractable_blob_path` (native text = the bytes as UTF-8, v4's
`Buffer.toString('utf-8')` = `String::from_utf8_lossy`); the NEW `_indexed`
upload bridges (contract C1) that extract + chunk + enqueue for the seven
callers (five switched here, the two restore sites handed to P4.D355); the
two database-store readers + the repository worklist query; the NEW boot
backfill (contract C2) called ONCE from the host's mount-index boot closure —
idempotent, stampless, never throwing, SKIPPING `pdf` / `docx` while the seam
refuses (R-1); `doc_read_file`'s ENOENT fallback onto the blob's text AND
v4's pre-existing converted-blob `derivedFromBlob` branch (the `text.rs`
deferral retires); `doc_grep`'s blob pass after the documents loop;
`doc_list_files`' `pattern` as a real glob (NEW `doc_edit/path_glob.rs`,
compiled through `regress` for JS-dialect exactness — R-3) at the three
sites, matched against the RELATIVE path. (2) **`82a2f6536`** — the
MOUNT_CHUNK embedding handler checks `partition_state(MountIndex)` BEFORE
the chunk read and fails the JOB (a retry) with v4's two texts, instead of
failing the chunk forever. (3) **`faeae2b71`** — the persistent-target check

`assert_persistent_target`, v4's five lines and the operator message
byte-exact) wired into `run_sync` before the dry-run branch through a
`MountInfoSource` seam on `SyncDeps` (C7) as `SyncRefusalCode::
TargetNotPersistent` → 400; the deconvert half recorded against the P4.6y
refusal; the browse-directory default = the instance root under Docker
(`CoreConfig.base_dir`) else home, with the new DEBUG line — ONE
`engine.rs` line; the CLI's `/app/quilltap` default inside a container with
v4's CLI probe (NO `/app` heuristic — R-6). (4) **`026222176`** — the WARN
byte move at the eleven sites, the IMPORT-DEAD oracle re-aimed by mocking
`convertPdfBufferToText`, the seam's doc carrying v4's assembly rules as the
expected lines of a future extractor, NO new PDF crate (R-2). Every unit

## Survey-verified starting points (2026-10-29, at `236c04e35`; v5 `main` `f6cdceedd`)

### The v4 hunks, file by file (what they DO)

**`40fab1c54` — bugs 260 / 261** (28 files; prod 11 files +495 / −99):

- `lib/mount-index/path-utils.ts:65-91`: `detectBlobFileType(relativePath):
  'pdf' | 'docx' | 'blob'` (`:69-74` — moved out of `store-file.ts`, same
  body: `path.extname().toLowerCase()`; `.pdf` → `pdf`, `.docx` → `docx`,
  else `blob`); `isExtractableBlobPath(relativePath)` (`:81-83`) =
  `EXTRACTABLE_BLOB_EXTENSIONS.includes(path.extname(relativePath).
  toLowerCase())`; `EXTRACTABLE_BLOB_EXTENSIONS: readonly string[]`
  (`:89-91`) = `['.md', '.markdown', '.txt', '.json', '.jsonl', '.ndjson',
  '.pdf', '.docx']` — a test holds it to `detectNativeText`'s set + the two
  converter types (`extractable-blob-paths.test.ts:24-31`).
- `lib/mount-index/store-file.ts:158-239` NEW exported `extractBlobText({
  blobId, linkId, relativePath, data, mountPointId }): Promise<boolean>`:
  - `:166` `if (!isExtractableBlobPath(relativePath)) return false;`
  - `:169-173` `blobType = detectBlobFileType(relativePath)`; text =
    `pdf` / `docx` → `await convertBufferToPlainText(data, blobType)`, else
    `data.toString('utf-8')`.
  - `:174-196` non-blank text (`text && text.trim().length > 0`) →
    `docMountBlobs.updateExtractedText(blobId, { extractedText: text,

  `.then(backfillUploadBlobText)` and a catch WARN `Uploaded-blob text

- `lib/doc-edit/path-glob.ts` (NEW, 98, pure): `globToRegExpSource`
  (`:25-69`) and `compilePathGlob(pattern)` (`:76-98`). The rules, verbatim
  from the hunk: normalise = `trim()`, `\` → `/`, strip leading `./`
  repeats (`/^(?:\.\/)+/`) then leading `/`s; empty → matches everything;
  `matchesBasename = !normalised.includes('/')` — a slash-less pattern tests
  the LAST segment (`p.split('/').pop() ?? ''`), otherwise the whole path
  (the input also `\` → `/` and leading `/`s stripped); `**` at a segment
  start (`i === 0 || glob[i-1] === '/'`) followed by `/` → `(?:[^/]*/)*`
  (the `/` consumed); any other `**` → `.*`; `*` → `[^/]*`; `?` → `[^/]`;
  `[` with a closing `]` → `[${body}]` with a leading `!` → `^` and
  backslashes doubled, an unclosed `[` → `\[`; `{` → `(?:` (depth++), `}`
  at depth > 0 → `)`, `,` at depth > 0 → `|`; every other char escaped via
  `/[.+^${}()|[\]\\]/g` → `\$&`; unclosed braces closed with `)` × depth;
  `new RegExp('^' + src + '$', 'i')`; a RegExp that fails to compile →
  exact case-insensitive literal compare on the chosen target.
- `lib/tools/handlers/doc-edit/text-handlers.ts`: (a) `:85-102` NEW
  `readTextFileOrUploadedBlob(resolved)` — `readFileWithMtime`; on
  `code === 'ENOENT'` only, and only when `mountType === 'database' &&
  mountPointId`, `readDatabaseBlobText(mountPointId, relativePath)`; `null`
  → rethrow; else DEBUG `doc_read_file: no document at path; reading the
  uploaded blob's text` `{mountPointId, relativePath, size}` → `{content,
  mtime, size}`. (b) `:125-166` `handleReadFile`'s non-text branch — a
  database-backed path whose blob has converted text answers `{ content:
  outputLines.join('\n'), mimeType: blob.storedMimeType, path, uri, mtime:
  blob.mtime, totalLines, truncated, derivedFromBlob: true }` with
  `formattedText` = header `File: ${input.path} (extracted text from
  ${blob.storedMimeType}, ${totalLines} lines, ${blob.size} original
  bytes)` + the truncation note `\n[Truncated — showing lines
  ${lineStart}-${lineStart + outputLines.length - 1} of ${totalLines}]` +
  `\n\n` + `[L<n>] <line>` lines (offset / limit honoured, `truncated =
  endLine < lines.length` — this branch DOES slice `content`, unlike the
  text branch); otherwise `{ success: false, error: 'File is not a
  supported text format: <path>' }`. (c) `:854-864` `handleGrep`'s database
  branch: after the documents loop, `documentPaths = new Set(documents.map(d
  => d.relativePath.toLowerCase()))`; `listDatabaseBlobTexts(mp.id)`; per
  blob: `maxResults` break, skip when `documentPaths.has(blob.relativePath.
  toLowerCase())`, the `input.path` prefix filter, `isBlocked`, then
  `searchContent(blob.content, blob.relativePath, mp.name, makeUri)`. (d)
  `:959-962` `matchesPattern = input.pattern ? compilePathGlob(input.
  pattern) : null`; the three sites (`:997` fs walk, `:1073` database store,
  `:1122` official project store) test **`relativePath`** /
  `entry.relativePath` (was `entry.name` / `entry.fileName`).
- `lib/tools/doc-list-files-tool.ts:35` — the model-visible `pattern`
  description, byte-exact: `Optional: glob pattern to filter results (e.g.,
  "*.md", "*.{ts,tsx}", "src/**/*.js"). A pattern with no "/" matches file
  names in any folder; "**/" spans any number of folders, including none.
  Case-insensitive.`
- help ×2 (`help/document-editing-tools.md:70-72`, `help/file-uploads.md:
  71-80`) — P4.D360's (H3).

**`82a2f6536` — the mount-chunk guard** (10 files; prod 3): the ONE hunk

(`lib/database/backends/sqlite/mount-index-guard.ts:17-29`, unchanged at
the pin) throws `Mount index database is in degraded mode` when
`isMountIndexDegraded()`, else `Mount index database not initialized` when
the raw handle is null. The throw propagates out of the handler → the job

`child-startup.ts` (the forked child's DB-before-plugins order): NO-PORT
(v5's in-process runner shares the host's open connections).

**`026222176` — pdfjs-dist 6.4 + the in-house reader** (25 files; nearly
all `public/pdf.worker.mjs` and rebuilt plugin bundles):

- `lib/mount-index/converters/pdf-converter.ts` (post-hunk, 125 lines):
  `LINE_THRESHOLD = 4.6`, `CELL_THRESHOLD = 7`, `CELL_SEPARATOR = '\t'`
  ("pdf-parse 2.x's defaults … keeps already-indexed documents byte-stable
  on re-index"); `extractPageText(page)` (`:24-61`): `viewport =
  page.getViewport({scale: 1})`; per `textContent.items` item with `str`:
  `[x, y] = viewport.convertToViewportPoint(item.transform[4],
  item.transform[5])`; with a previous item: `yDiff = |lastY − y|`; `yDiff >
  4.6` → when the last part exists, does not end `\n`, the item does not
  start its own line (`str.startsWith('\n') || (str.trim() === '' &&
  item.hasEOL)`) and `yDiff − 1 > lineHeight` → push `'\n'`, `lineHeight =
  0`; else `yDiff < 4.6 && lastX !== undefined && |lastX − x| > 7` → `str =
  '\t' + str`; push `str`; `lastX = x + item.width`; `lastY = y`;
  `lineHeight = max(lineHeight, item.height)`; `item.hasEOL` → push `'\n'`;
  `hasEOL || str.endsWith('\n')` → `lineHeight = 0`. `convertPdfBufferToText(
  buffer)` (`:69-110`): empty buffer → WARN `PDF buffer is empty` → `''`;
  `getDocument({ data: new Uint8Array(buffer), enableXfa: false, verbosity:
  VerbosityLevel.ERRORS })`; per page `1..numPages`: `text += pageText +
  '\n\n-- ${n} of ${numPages} --\n\n'`, `page.cleanup()` in a `finally`;
  DEBUG `Extracted PDF text` `{pages, chars}`; the catch → WARN `Failed to
  extract text from PDF buffer` `{error}` → `''`; `loadingTask?.destroy()`
  always.
- `lib/services/file-content-extractor.ts:175`: the WARN text moves —
  `pdf-parse found no text, using native fallback extraction` → **`PDF
  reader found no text, using native fallback extraction`** (`{size}`
  unchanged); `:167` "the one PDF text reader".
- `components/files/FilePreview/FilePreviewPdf.tsx` — bug 266 (a
  `renderTaskRef`; cancel + await the in-flight render; the cleanup cancels):
  **NO-PORT** — the v5 SPA previews a PDF as a download prompt
  (`apps/web/src/app/screens/files/file-preview-modal.ts:39`, `:168-178`
  "PDF preview isn't available yet — download to read it.").
- `package.json` / lockfile: `pdf-parse` REMOVED, `pdfjs-dist ^6.4.299`
  (+ `openai`, `@openrouter/sdk`, `next`, `katex` — P4.D360's guards).
  `next.config.js` / `scripts/build-standalone-overlay.mjs` externalise
  `pdfjs-dist` — NO-PORT.

  mount-index boot write closure (`if let Some(mi) = ws.mount_index() {` at

- **The glob: NO-COUNTERPART.** `tools/doc_edit/text.rs:1482-1490`

- **`tools/doc_edit/text.rs::handle_read_file :74`:** the non-text arm

## Tiered deliverables

### Tier 1 — must land (the `FILES` commit, or `FILES` + the immediately following commits — state which)

1. **Red-first, before any source edit** (§R.7), counts in the record:

     `list_glob_bare_name_nested` finds the nested file on v4 only; the
     brace / class / mixed-case rows RED; `read_uploaded_md_blob` answers

     answers the content; `grep_upload_only` 0 matches vs 1;
     `read_converted_pdf_blob` (a planted `extractedText` row) answers v5's
     `File is not a supported text format: …` vs v4's `derivedFromBlob`
     body. Record the row names and the two bodies per row.

     the `.md` / `.txt` / `.json` upload rows (`extractText: true,

     extractionStatus` NULL / `conversionStatus` NULL and `doc_mount_chunks`

     (item 13):** on `main` v5 writes an `embedding_status` row FAILED

     lists `$HOME` where v4 lists the data dir — RED.
   - **`file_content_extractor_equivalence` (item 18):** on the pin the
     oracle case DIES at import (`Cannot find module 'pdf-parse'` — record
     the exact text); after the re-aim and before the Rust move, every PDF

   - **`qtap_import_equivalence` + `files_routes_equivalence` regenerated

     equivalence`, `upload_blob_text_backfill_equivalence`) cannot run
     against `main` — their red-first is a MUTATION run, recorded: the glob
     with `**/` compiled as `.*` (the `**/CHANGELOG.md` → `docs/
     OLD-CHANGELOG.md` row flips TRUE); the basename rule dropped (the
     `*.md` → `deep/folder/notes.md` row flips); case-sensitive compile (the

6. **`services/mount_index/upload_blob_text_backfill.rs` (NEW) — contract
   C2.** `pub struct UploadBlobTextBackfillResult { pub candidates: usize,
   pub extracted: usize, pub failed: usize, pub stores_touched: usize }`
   (`Serialize` camelCase for the log bag) and `pub fn
   backfill_upload_blob_text(main: &Connection, mount: &Connection,
   extractor: &dyn DocumentTextExtractor) -> UploadBlobTextBackfillResult`
   — NEVER errors (every `DbError` inside becomes the outer WARN). Body in
   v4's order with v4's lines under `target: "quilltap::startup"` (R-10):
   worklist = `list_unextracted_by_extensions(&extensions)` where
   `extensions` = `EXTRACTABLE_BLOB_EXTENSIONS` when `extractor.is_
   available()`, else the SIX native-text extensions ONLY (R-1 — the
   pdf/docx rows are never listed, so `candidates` excludes them; a v5-only
   INFO `Leaving PDF/DOCX uploads unextracted; no document text extractor is
   available` `{skippedExtensions: [".pdf", ".docx"]}` fires ONCE per boot
   when the reduced list is used — named as v5's line); empty → DEBUG `No
   unextracted text blobs; nothing to backfill`, return; INFO `Extracting
   text from uploaded blobs` `{candidates}`; per blob: `read_data(&blob.id)`
   → `Ok(None)` or `Err` → WARN `Blob bytes missing; skipping text backfill`
   `{mountPointId, relativePath}`, `failed += 1`, continue;
   `extract_blob_text(mount, &args, extractor, LeaveUnextracted)` false →
   `failed += 1`, continue; else `extracted += 1`, `touched.insert(mp)`,
   `reindex_single_file(mount, mp, rel, "", extractor)` (best-effort; v4's
   WARN `Chunking a backfilled blob failed; its text is still readable` is
   UNREACHABLE — recorded in the module doc, not emitted); per touched store
   (in first-touched order — v4's `Set` iteration order; a `BTreeSet` would
   NOT match, use an insertion-ordered `Vec` + `contains`):
   `enqueue_embedding_jobs_for_mount_point(main, mount, mp)` then
   `refresh_stats(mp)`; an `Err` from either → WARN `Queueing embeddings for
   a backfilled store failed` `{mountPointId, error}`; `stores_touched =
   touched.len()`; INFO `Uploaded-blob text backfill finished` `{candidates,
   extracted, failed, storesTouched}`; the outer catch → WARN `Uploaded-blob
   text backfill failed` `{error, candidates, extracted, failed,
   storesTouched}` (reachable in v5 only through a `DbError` in the
   worklist / per-store writes — route every `?` inside to it). Register
   `pub mod upload_blob_text_backfill;` in `services/mount_index/mod.rs`
   (alphabetical, after `sync`). **The host call — ONE hunk in
   `crates/quilltap-host/src/host.rs`,** inside the `if let Some(mi) =
   ws.mount_index() {` closure, immediately after the `ensure_builtin_
   mounts_with(...)` call at `:1971-1975` and before `// === P4.171 (C-19)`:
   ```rust
   // === P4.D354 (v4 `40fab1c54`, bugs 260 / 261): uploads stored before
   // text extraction get what new uploads get at ingest. v4 chains this
   // after PHASE 3.3's `scanAllMountPoints`; v5 has no boot scan, so it
   // runs here, after the built-in mounts exist and before the scenario
   // heals. Idempotent, stampless, never fails the boot.
   let extractor = quilltap_core::services::mount_index::converters::default_text_extractor();
   let _ = quilltap_core::services::mount_index::upload_blob_text_backfill::backfill_upload_blob_text(
       main, mount_index, extractor.as_ref(),
   );
   // === end P4.D354 ===
   ```

7. **`doc_edit/path_glob.rs` (NEW) + `pub mod path_glob;` in
   `doc_edit/mod.rs` — R-3.** `pub fn glob_to_regexp_source(glob: &str) ->
   String` (v4's `globToRegExpSource`, char by char over UTF-16 units? — NO:
   v4 indexes `glob[i]` by UTF-16 code unit, but every branch tests ASCII
   chars and passes others through `.replace(/[.+^${}()|[\]\\]/g, '\\$&')`,
   which is the identity on non-ASCII; iterate Rust `chars()` and prove the
   equivalence with an astral-plane row — `📁/*.md`) and `pub struct
   PathGlob` with `pub fn compile(pattern: &str) -> PathGlob` and `pub fn
   matches(&self, relative_path: &str) -> bool`: the normalisation
   (`js_trim`, `\` → `/`, `^(?:\./)+` stripped, `^/+` stripped), the empty
   → `MatchAll`, `matches_basename = !normalised.contains('/')`, the
   `regress::Regex::with_flags(&format!("^{src}$"), "i")` compile — on
   `Err` the `Literal(lowercased)` arm comparing `to_lowercase()` of the
   chosen target (JS `toLowerCase` = `str::to_lowercase`, byte-identical —
   CLAUDE.md's Phase-1 ICU record); `matches` normalises the input (`\` →
   `/`, `^/+` stripped), picks the last segment (`rsplit('/').next()`) when
   `matches_basename`, and runs `regress`'s `find` (anchored by the source).
   `regress` because v4's source is a JS RegExp: class bodies with `^`
   prefixes and doubled backslashes, `(?:…)` groups, the `i` flag over
   UTF-16 — the `regex` crate's dialect differs on `[` escapes and would
   need a translator (the alternative, rejected). Unit tests = the 8 `it`s
   of `path-glob.test.ts` (every `expect` a row) + the astral row + a
   `{a,b` unclosed row + an `[unclosed` row.

    fail-open WARN — which never fires outside Docker because the docker
    gate returns first. Recorded.

    home (item 12) — recorded, not stubbed.

32. **The help pages** (`document-editing-tools.md`, `file-uploads.md`,

## Rulings made at planning (the planner's calls; the human may overrule before lane start — a STOP item if they do)

Round rulings that govern this lane, by reference (§R.11): **THE PIN**
(`236c04e35`); **THE NPM CACHE GAP** (one online `npm ci` before the lanes;
`--offline` must succeed or STOP); **R-C** (NO new PDF crate; the seam's spec
updated; the backfill SKIPS pdf / docx while the seam refuses — a recorded
divergence); **R-E** (observable shifts, not file splits); **R-Z** (not this
round's: `8832cfd20`, `bf7a457f6`).

⚖ **Items the HUMAN rules first:**

- ⚖ **R-1 (round R-C, widened by one step) — the pdf / docx skip applies to
  the backfill AND to the new `_indexed` bridges, not to `store_mount_file`'s
  REST path.** v4's `extractBlobText` writes `extractionStatus 'failed'` /
  `Converter produced no text` when its converter answers `''`; under v5's
  refusing seam that would stamp EVERY PDF / DOCX upload — old (the backfill)
  and new (every chat upload) — `failed` forever, and v4's own worklist
  (`'none'` only) would never revisit them when a real extractor lands. The
  ruling: `extract_blob_text` takes `RefusedConverterPolicy`; the backfill
  and the bridges pass `LeaveUnextracted` (the row stays `'none'`, nothing
  written, a v5-only DEBUG / one INFO per boot); `store_mount_file` (the
  Scriptorium REST upload, where v5 ALREADY writes v4's `failed` bytes
  today and `mount_write_equivalence` pins them) passes `WriteFailed`. ONE
  function, both arms, a NAMED both-ways carve
  (`REFUSING_SEAM_LEAVES_PDF_UNEXTRACTED`, item 26). **The planner's R-C
  named the backfill only; the bridge half is the planner's extension —
  the human may narrow it to the backfill** (then the bridges pass
  `WriteFailed` and a chat-uploaded PDF reads as `failed`, which the SPA's
  file list shows as a conversion failure; record which). The alternative
  (v4's bytes everywhere) was rejected for the freeze-out above.
- ⚖ **R-2 (round R-C) — NO new PDF crate this round.** `converters.rs`'s
  doc carries v4's pdfjs-direct assembly rules as the expected lines; the
  options are recorded (`pdf-extract` / `pdfium-render`; `mupdf` unsuitable;
  DOCX a separate ruling). The human may instead order the extractor NOW —
  that is a NEW order (the seam widening + the catch arm + the carve
  retirement, H5), not a rider on this lane.

**The planner's calls for this lane:**

- **R-3 — the glob compiles through `regress`, not `regex`.** v4 emits a
  JS RegExp source (`[^…]` classes built from the glob's own `[!…]` body
  with backslashes doubled, `(?:…)` groups, the `i` flag, UTF-16 semantics);
  `regress` is the workspace's ECMAScript engine (`Cargo.toml:56`, already
  used for JS-dialect regexes) and compiles that source unchanged, so the
  Rust side ports the SOURCE GENERATOR byte-for-byte and the engine is
  v4's. Alternative rejected: a hand-written matcher (exactness on odd
  patterns — `[a-]`, `{,}`, `a**b` — would be re-derived, not ported) or
  the `regex` crate (a different class / escape dialect needs a translator
  the oracle would then have to prove).
- **R-4 — the persistence check's `is_docker` is v4's SERVER probe**
  (`services::data_dir::is_docker_environment` — WITH the `/app` directory
  heuristic, as v4's `lib/paths.ts` `isDockerEnvironment()` the module
  imports), read by `run_sync` through `DataDirEnv::from_process()` and
  passed in; the mountinfo bytes come through `SyncDeps.mount_info` (C7)
  so the harness injects a table. Alternative rejected: reading
  `/proc/self/mountinfo` inside the module with no seam (untestable on a
  Mac — the refusal path would never run in any test).
- **R-5 — the browse default decision lives in `api/system.rs`
  (`browse_default_dir(env, base_dir)`), and the engine passes
  `&self.inner.config.base_dir`.** ONE engine line; no `quilltap-host`
  dependency (the ledger row's pointer corrected). v4's `getBaseDataDir()`
  is the instance root = `CoreConfig.base_dir` (`engine.rs:61-63`).
- **R-6 — the CLI's docker probe is v4's CLI probe, not the server's.**
  `platform-paths.js` deliberately omits the `/app` heuristic ("misfires on
  hosts that happen to have /app"); `quilltap-cli` gets its own
  `is_docker_container()` (`DOCKER_CONTAINER === 'true' || /.dockerenv`),
  NOT `quilltap-host::env::is_docker_environment` (which carries `/app`).
  Two probes, two owners, as in v4.
- **R-7 — the mount-chunk guard returns `Err(String)` from
  `mount_chunk_branch`** with v4's two texts, mapped from
  `partition_state(MountIndex)`'s non-`Open` variants (degraded → `… in
  degraded mode`; absent → `… not initialized`); the job's existing failure
  channel carries it to the retry. Alternative rejected: a new error enum
  (the runner compares nothing but the string; v4's is a bare `Error`).
- **R-8 — the deprecated bridges stay ONE round** with
  `#[deprecated]` + the `bridge_callers_census` (item 4); the two restore
  sites switch in P4.D355 (H1); the pair is deleted by the unifier when the
  census reads zero deprecated callers, or by the next round. Alternative
  rejected: switching the restore sites yourself (P4.D355 owns the file this
  round) or changing the OLD functions' behaviour in place (the restore
  census dumps would move under P4.D355's feet before its regen).
- **R-9 — `refresh_now` is the ONE tail.** The `_indexed` bridges and
  `store_mount_file` share `refresh.rs:25`; the backfill calls its three
  parts separately ONLY because v4's backfill does (`reindexSingleFile` per
  blob; `enqueueEmbeddingJobsForMountPoint` + `refreshStats` per STORE — a
  different grouping, observable in the `background_jobs` row order).
- **R-10 — the backfill logs under `target: "quilltap::startup"`** (shared
  with `daily_db_optimize.rs` — v4's `Startup:*` service loggers' twin);
  the lines fire inside the host's `write_blocking` closure, so every pin
  uses `global_capture` (memory note `capture-rig-writer-thread`), and the
  record states the target by name.
- **R-11 — `doc_read_file`'s converted-blob branch ports WHOLE** (v4's
  pre-existing `:125-166`, not just the new ENOENT fallback): bug 260's read
  arm cannot be exercised without it (a converted PDF upload is the branch's
  own case), and the deferral's premise ("the corpus reads only text files")
  ends with the grown `doc_enum` rows. The `derivedFromBlob` key order is
  v4's literal.
- **R-12 — `DocumentTextExtractor::is_available()` is a DEFAULTED trait
  method** (additive; only `RefusingTextExtractor` overrides it `false`), so
  no implementor in the harness or the host moves and the R-1 policy reads
  the seam rather than a global. Alternative rejected: a `cfg` / env switch
  (invisible to the carve) or downcasting.
- **R-13 — no `Cargo.lock` move, no new dependency** anywhere in this lane
  (`regress` and `regex` are present); a lockfile diff is a STOP.

    FLAG, not a tier (R-E) — `bucketCandidates` skips `memory.keystone`
    rows AFTER `rowsLoaded++`, BEFORE the `CONSOLIDATED` branch; `isDemotable
    = hot && AUTO && !keystone`; `policyProtectionOf` answers `'keystone'`
    FIRST and `sparedBy`'s key order becomes `{keystone, manual, digest,
    cold}`; a keystone fold survivor takes NO footnotes and an EMPTY patch
    but keeps links + the reinforcement delta; `cascadeEmptiedDigests` keeps
    a keystone digest whose last member retires (DEBUG `Keystone digest kept
    though its last member retires`) and its `added` now counts only rows
    actually doomed (`if (!grew) break`) — port as shipped; `protection()`
    answers `{score: 1, protected: true, protectedBySource: true}`. In
    recall, "every digest is cut before any keystone" holds only INSIDE the
    inner circle (`innerCircleTrimOrder` sorts `Number(b.keystone) −
    Number(a.keystone)` first): present-character keystones are LIFTED into
    the circle at `tier −1` ahead of that character's digests, self
    keystones join the anchors, and `renderArchive` / `renderHead` render a
    keystone's `content` first (`summary` first otherwise). `KEYSTONES_PER_
    SUBJECT_MAX = 5`; the v4 quirk — `POST /memories` with `keystone: true`
    answers 400 `The memory was saved, but not as a keystone: <msg>` AFTER
    writing the row — is ported as the bytes (Q3). `keystone` appears in
    EVERY parsed Memory (between `consolidatedAt` and `createdAt`), so every
    byte pin of a memory row moves; `hydrateRawRows` coerces number / bigint
    / null to a boolean and DROPS a string cell with the WARN.

    own commit; the unifier checks the handoff list. Ties go to the feature
    lane (the a11y edits are mechanical)." Every sibling SPA lane applies
    rules **R1–R12** (the binding text is Shared contract C9) to the files it
    owns and records each as a `HANDOFF:` for P4.D358's census; P4.D358's
    static census script takes an allowlist for sibling-owned files, which
    the unifier empties. Already CONVERGED (not reds): the sidebar switches,
    `Allow Any Character`, the edit box, the composer `Message`, the

    `role="dialog"`; `qt-modal` (77 hosts) carries `role` + `aria-modal` +

- **§R.5 — Log lines.** Every line this round ports or moves lands with a
  capture pin (the thread-scoped rig) AND its silence leg.
  - A line emitted INSIDE a `Db::write` closure runs on the writer thread and
    is INVISIBLE to the rig: run the home on the caller thread (memory note
    `capture-rig-writer-thread`) or use `global_capture`.
  - A `db::fallback` line renders the BARE SQLite / Zod message
    (`error_text`), never `sqlite error: …`.
  - A structured v4 field that is an array or object renders through the
    `…Json` file-layer convention; v4's camelCase keys are the keys; an
    `undefined` v4 field is OMITTED; child-logger bindings are fields on the
    line; `durationMs` and other wall-clock fields are MASKED.
  - Open before `captured_with`; a v4 catch UNREACHABLE through v4's real
    code is pinned NEGATIVE with a v5 fallback home.
  - **Migration lines follow the standing precedent** (P4.D249 / P4.D304 /
    P4.D315 / P4.D324, `host.rs`): v4's migration INFO / ERROR texts are
    NO-PORT and each boot ensure logs ONE v5-worded INFO per added column /
    table / index.

- **§R.7 — Red-first.**
  - **Measure red before touching source** — on unported `main` for the
    wave-0 lanes, on the `KEYSTONE` tip for its dependents (a contract
    refusal arm answering `not_available` IS the red). The lane record
    carries the red count per family. A test that passes before the fix is
    a test of nothing.
  - **Ruled divergences** are NAMED both-ways tables in the idiom of
    `FRESH_STORE_RESIDUAL` / `BOOT_CREATED_HISTORY_TABLE`, so a v4
    convergence trips them. This round's planned names: **P4.D345**
    `HOLD_KEEP_ALIVE_NO_STREAM` (v4's 15 s keep-alive ping — no v5
    counterpart by architecture), `ROUND_DROPS_MIN_EXCLUSIVE` (only if the
    corpus shows it); **P4.D354** `REFUSING_SEAM_LEAVES_PDF_UNEXTRACTED`
    (the backfill and the `_indexed` bridges leave a PDF / DOCX row at
    `'none'` while `DocumentTextExtractor::is_available()` is `false`; the
    REST path keeps v4's `failed` bytes); **P4.D347** `ECHO_SLICE_SURROGATE`
    + `SENTENCE_SLICE_SURROGATE` (a JS `slice` that cuts a surrogate pair —
    v5 rounds to the code-point boundary); **P4.D343** the dry scope's
    table-delta proof (whatever the `DryBuildContextSeams` wrapper does not
    cover is a recorded divergence, never a silent write) and `plugin_tool_
    effects` as NO-COUNTERPART; **P4.D350** `PUBLISH_AFTER_COMMIT` only if
    the human overrules its R-1 (v4 publishes at the write; v5 after the
    commit); **P4.D355** the explicit `terminal_sessions` DELETE on chat
    delete (v5's cascade stand-in on a generateDDL table with no FK — named
    in its record; R-R); **P4.D341** none owed (Q3's post-write 400 is
    ported as bytes); **P4.D352** an ADAPTER divergence recorded in the SPA
    (ProseMirror `Decoration.inline` for v4's CSS Custom Highlight API; the
    four `::highlight(name)` rules become class rules with the same tokens);
    **P4.D353** a Tier-3 code-block note only if R-B is overruled to (C).
  - **BY-DESIGN flips (ledger §5.4 — measure, then retire / re-aim, never
    delete unread):** the #120 convergence pins (**P4.D356** — the three
    `s_get_cheap_llm_bad_strategy` / `_bad_uuid` / `_non_object` rows
    compared whole at `settings_routes_equivalence.rs:591-599`, the unit test
    `update_for_user_mints_a_new_row_past_a_refused_first_row` at

- **§R.8 — The unifier's accumulation.** Each lane's record states its
  `Cargo.toml` versions at close; the unifier takes the HIGHEST and bumps +1
  for the union commit; `Cargo.lock` follows. The SPA: the highest of the
  SPA lanes, +1. CHANGELOG entries and `status-log.md` lane records
  union-merge (append-only).
- **§R.9 — One machine.**
  - **Workspace gates:** at most TWO Rust workspace gates at once. Count them
    with ONE `ps -ax -o pid,etime,command | grep "cargo test --workspace" |
    grep -v grep`; if two run, queue yours in ONE background command waiting
    on the siblings' PIDs (`while kill -0 <pidA> && kill -0 <pidB>; do sleep
    20; done; sleep $((RANDOM % 20)); <re-count once; if still two, wait on
    those PIDs>; cargo test …`), never on a pattern (memory notes
    `no-pattern-kill-on-shared-machine`, `parallel-lane-shared-resources`
    §2 — co-waiters all fire on one exit; re-count after the wait).
  - **Sweeps and Playwright:** one sweep at a time (announced); one
    Playwright run at a time (lanes by file only; the full suite is the
    unifier's). New own-instance e2e files' server ports are unique per
    lane (4319–4340, 4343–4350, 4352 and mocks 45301–45304, 45340–45348 are
    taken): **P4.D344 4353 / mock 45351**, **P4.D346 4354 / mock 45352**,
    **P4.D352 4355 / mock 45353**, **P4.D353 4356 / mock 45354**, **P4.D358
    4357 / mock 45355**, **P4.D359 4358 / mock 45356**, **P4.D355 4359 /
    mock 45357** (only if a terminal e2e is added). A lane that only edits
    an EXISTING spec takes no port.
  - **Long jobs:** `run_in_background: true`, full log in a lane-private
    file, awaited by the completion notification — NEVER a `pgrep`/`grep`
    poll loop (CLAUDE.md), never `tail -N` on a gate log. A gate reads its
    oracles and source at RUN time: no regen and no source edit while one
    runs (memory note `lane-gate-hygiene`).
  - **Targets:** clone main's `target/` before the first build (`cp -cR`;
    `/carryout` rule 8 — same volume only, memory note
    `apfs-clone-same-volume-only`); main's `target/` was warmed by the
    `/setupphase` chain on 2026-10-29 at the orders' commit (the test /
    clippy set the lanes clone; the release bins were swept — an
    own-instance e2e that prefers `target/release/*` must delete the stale
    release bins from its clone or build release, memory note
    `lane-spa-env-node-modules-and-release-bins`); a stacked lane may clone
    its parent lane's worktree target instead, when that lane is not
    mid-build.
  - **`apps/web/dist/`:** a fresh worktree has none — build it before any
    workspace clippy / test. **`apps/web/node_modules`:** `npm ci` in the
    lane (a cloned copy fails every vitest spec with NG0203).
  - **Concurrency:** the cap is the two-gate rule, not the lane count;
    twenty lanes do NOT all run at once (§S.0 waves). Disk at planning:
    144 GiB free on the repo volume with `target/` at 347 GB BEFORE the
    warm sweep — the human sizes the waves from the post-sweep numbers in
    the hand-off report.

  (2026-10-29): the npm-cache step (ONE online `npm ci` in a pin worktree

- **§R.14 — Stacking rules.** Each named stacking commit — `KEYSTONE`
  (P4.D341), `CONTEXT` (P4.D347), `SPINE` (P4.D348), `CATALOG` (P4.D350's
  FIRST commit), `FILES` (P4.D354) — is ONE commit whose subject starts with
  its name; the owner records the sha in its status header ON ITS LANE
  BRANCH the moment it lands. A dependent lane — P4.D347 / P4.D342 / P4.D351
  / P4.D356 from `KEYSTONE`; P4.D348 from `CONTEXT`; P4.D343 / P4.D345 from
  `SPINE` (which is on `CONTEXT` on `KEYSTONE`); P4.D349 from `CATALOG`;
  P4.D355 from `FILES` — finds the sha per `/carryout` rule 2 (`git log --all
  --format='%h %s' | grep -E '^[0-9a-f]+ <NAME>:'`, confirmed against the
  parent's lane-branch copy of the order) and branches from it — never from
  `main`, never a guessed sha; if the parent has not recorded it, STOP and
  report. A stacked lane may PRE-START from `main` on work that calls none
  of its parent's contract names — P4.D342's prompt bytes (the four
  extraction ladders + the consolidation tail) + the inner-circle resolver
  (`services/inner_circle.rs`, the tuning knobs); P4.D343's `tools/effects.rs`
  + `pascal/tool_effect.rs` + `StreamParams.tool_choice` + the `tool-build`
  re-aim (its unit E) + the pure `storyteller/*` modules; P4.D345's hold
  registry + eligibility / overrides / recompute / spans + the engine's
  `ReplayInputs` + the ONE corpus regen + the schema re-vendor; P4.D351's
  `services/annotations.rs` + `template_prompt_hint.rs` ports; P4.D356's
  NanoGPT half + the Tier R verify; P4.D349's `pronoun-presets` /
  `pronoun-guidance` ports + the image-prompt `humanoid` threading; P4.D355's
  `db/log_entries.rs` + the terminal host; P4.D348's `operator_post.rs` /
  `whisper_send.rs` / `turn_control.rs` additions and the funnel recorder;
  P4.D347 has no pre-start (its first unit widens `RawMessage`, which rides
  the keystone's row shapes) — rebasing onto the parent's commit before the
  first commit that does call a contract name; that rebase is the one
  sanctioned exception to "no rebase mid-lane". Two sibling-stacked parts
  (not lane-named commits): P4.D346's grammar-twin part (`pascal/custom-tool-
  types.ts` + the corpus copy + the spec header) stacks on P4.D345's Tier-1
  commit, or authors blind and lets the unifier run `custom-tool-types.
  corpus.spec.ts` — the order states which; P4.D359's chips stack on
  P4.D353's Tier-1 commit (the `message-content.ts` hook). Every other lane
  cuts from `main` at the orders' commit.

## §S — Unifier wires (what no single lane can do)

- **§S.0 — Waves (the recommended execution; the two-gate cap rules the
  machine, not the lane count).**
  - **Wave 0, from the orders' commit, at once:** P4.D341 (→ `KEYSTONE`,
    the Opus / Fable tier), P4.D350 (→ `CATALOG` as its FIRST commit, then
    its own units), P4.D354 (→ `FILES`), and in parallel P4.D360, P4.D357,
    P4.D358. That is six worktrees.
  - **Wave 1, as slots free (still from the orders' commit):** the five SPA
    feature lanes P4.D344, P4.D346, P4.D352, P4.D353, P4.D359 (each authors
    against the contracts with gated beats; P4.D346's grammar-twin part and
    P4.D359's chips wait for their sibling's Tier-1 commit).
  - **Wave A, from `KEYSTONE`:** P4.D347 (→ `CONTEXT`) FIRST, then P4.D342,
    P4.D351, P4.D356 (its NanoGPT + Tier R half may have PRE-STARTED from
    `main`). **From `CATALOG`:** P4.D349 — FIRST among the independents
    (R-H). **From `FILES`:** P4.D355.
  - **Wave B, from `CONTEXT`:** P4.D348 (→ `SPINE`).
  - **Wave C, from `SPINE`:** P4.D343 and P4.D345 (the two turn-side
    consumers every later union depends on).

  Within a wave every lane compiles against the C sections below it alone.
- **§S.1 — The pick order.** P4.D341 → P4.D350 → P4.D349 → P4.D347 →
  P4.D348 → P4.D343 → P4.D345 → P4.D342 → P4.D351 → P4.D356 → P4.D354 →
  P4.D355 → P4.D357 → P4.D344 → P4.D346 → P4.D352 → P4.D353 → P4.D359 →
  P4.D360 → P4.D358. The reasons per adjacency: `KEYSTONE` first (every
  server lane's row shapes, the dumps, the ensures); P4.D350 next because
  `CATALOG` is the base P4.D349 branches from, and P4.D349 right after it
  (R-H — the three LIVE-DATA erasure writers land before anything else
  touches a vault); P4.D347 → P4.D348 → P4.D343 → P4.D345 in their stacking
  order (`CONTEXT` on `KEYSTONE`, `SPINE` on `CONTEXT`, the two consumers of
  S1 / S2 / C1 / `TurnStopReach` on `SPINE`; P4.D343 before P4.D345 so
  `trigger_runner.rs`'s pre-run ceiling block precedes the post-run `Vetoed`
  arm and `realtime/types.rs` lands `storytellerRounds` NINTH before
  `customToolInterventions` TENTH); P4.D342 and P4.D351 (keystone-only
  consumers, no spine seam) after the spine chain; P4.D356 after P4.D345 so
  the `customToolIntervention` arm it writes for P4.D345 and the
  `punctuationWarnings` key it writes for P4.D352 are in the tree when their
  families re-run, and after P4.D351 so `settings-routes` is regenerated
  ONCE at the end of the server picks; P4.D354 → P4.D355 (`FILES` then the
  two bridge sites switched); P4.D357 before every SPA lane (the CSS lines
  the fenced appends sit beside); P4.D344 before P4.D346 (`_index.css`
  imports in v4's order; `RealtimeTopicAppend`'s SPA half NINTH then TENTH;
  `LibraryEntryKeys`' SPA badges in v4's order); P4.D352 before P4.D353
  (`renderMarkdownToHtml` is P4.D352's; P4.D353 post-passes its HTML);
  P4.D353 before P4.D359 (P4.D359's chips stack on P4.D353's `message-
  content.ts` hook; its `_variables.css` append sits after P4.D353's);
  P4.D360 LATE (its help / docs guards judge a union carrying every server
  feature; its schema-guard CHECK sees the final constants; the P4.D339
  precedent: fourteenth) — nineteenth here, before P4.D358; P4.D358 LAST
  (its census allowlist for sibling-owned HANDOFF files is emptied against
  the finished union; its toast-selector first commit is cherry-picked
  EARLIER by any sibling that edits one of the 20 spec files).

  §1 is restated at the close probe: the regen rule (PIN REQUIRED at the
  new baseline — v4 HEAD is already past it); the schema state (moves
  17–21 LANDED in ONE re-dump; `migration_indexes.json` +3); the LIVE-DATA
  block (vendor survey §I, sharpened by the keystone survey — **a v5 at
  `236c04e35` reads and writes:** `memories.keystone` (the 17th move, the
  migration's `INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0` + the partial index `idx_memories_
  keystone` as ONE boot ensure), `chats.turnMode` / `storytellerInviteFilter`,
  `chat_messages.storytellerBid`, the NEW table `storyteller_rounds` (with
  its FK + both indexes as ensures), `llm_logs.storytellerRoundId` (the
  FIRST llm-logs column ensure v5 has needed — before the structural
  verify), `chat_settings.customToolIntervention` (default ON), `roleplay_
  templates.unmarkedStyle` / `markdownAsterisks` — and runs `778b97b2b`'s
  every-boot seed that re-encodes the built-in templates' `narrationDelimiters`
  as JSON and inserts the Novel template with it `null`. **The first v5
  boot of a pre-`8cb584eff` Friday copy ADDS the eight columns + one table
  (column-gated, v4's shapes, every ensure UNSTAMPED) and REWRITES the
  built-in templates' delimiter cells `*` → `"*"`; a copy v4 migrated sees
  NO-OP ensures and a no-op seed (measure: the seed must be idempotent
  against v4's own output).** **The THREE vault-key ERASURE hazards RETIRE**
  once P4.D349 lands: `render_physical_prompts_json` carries `humanoid` (a
  v4 `"humanoid": false` survives a v5 physical write and the P4.82
  backfill respects it), `PropertiesJson` carries `canDrawDiagrams` /
  `diagramStyle`, `build_wardrobe_item_file` carries `pronounGuidance`;
  until that lane is on the union build, **no v5 write to a character v4
  edited past `6588972d5` / `b27e5089a` / `eb90d9ed8`** (reads stay safe).
  **The Log is carried** (`cc5f2347c`, P4.D355): a v5 backup writes
  `data/log-entries.json`, a `replace` restore / delete-all clears
  `log_entries`, a `new-account` restore remaps `id` / `chatId` /
  `characterId`; `.qtap` still omits it; a pre-round v5 backup has no Log
  file — restoring it on the union leaves the Log empty (expected).
  **Keystones protected:** maintenance, demotion, consolidation, fold and
  chat-wipe skip `keystone = 1` rows; recall lifts them into the circle; a
  v5 backup / `.qtap` carries the column; a pre-round v5 build must NOT
  run maintenance on a copy v4 keystoned. **Terminals:** kill sends SIGHUP
  then SIGKILL after 3 s; a deleted chat terminates its shells (and v5
  deletes its `terminal_sessions` rows explicitly); the daily sweep unlinks
  orphaned transcripts older than 1 h (`85c002822`, P4.D355). **Settings:**
  the chat-settings PUT refuses unknown keys with a 400 (`3b8b6f42b`) — a
  v5 SPA caller that echoed a GET row back now fails (P4.D356's census).
  **Files:** ingest fills `doc_mount_blobs.extractedText*` for native-text
  uploads and a boot backfill fills old rows (`40fab1c54`, P4.D354 — a DATA
  WRITE on first boot of any copy with uploads: measure the row count; PDF
  / DOCX rows stay `'none'` under the refusing seam). **The general rule
  BITES: a v5 build from BEFORE this round must not open an instance this
  round's ensures have altered — the owed dogfood pass runs with the UNION
  build.** **NOT this round (`8832cfd20`, `bf7a457f6`):** v4 HEAD links
  SQLite3MC 2.4.0 / SQLite 3.53.4; v5 stays 2.3.5 / 3.53.2 — the first v5
  open of a post-`8832cfd20` Friday copy is the measurement; the iCloud
  placeholder scan divergence stands as recorded. **Standing:** no v5 build
  against live Friday; dogfood a COPY; do not round-trip v4-written
  characters through v5 writes while comparing with v4); the dependency
  facts (`openai` 7.45.0, `@openrouter/sdk` 1.5.9, `pdfjs-dist` 6.4.299,
  `pdf-parse` removed, `@quilltap/plugin-types` 2.9.0; the four client deps
  `mermaid` 12.2.0 / `@hpcc-js/wasm-graphviz` 1.30.0 / `svg2roughjs` 3.2.3 /
  `dompurify` 3.4.17 if R-B lands them; `@axe-core/playwright` if R-N does);
  `help/` 137; `docs/v4` 518.

- **§S.9 — The gated beats flip LIVE.** P4.D344 / P4.D346 / P4.D352 /

  (`const P4D343_SERVER_LANDED = false` etc.), with throwaway stand-ins

## Shared contracts (binding; name-level — the long forms are the surveys' §7 and, where an order is finished, that order's contract section)

- **C1 — the KEYSTONE surface P4.D341 lands at `KEYSTONE`** (keystone

  - **C-1 — the keystone reads (consumed by P4.D342):** `db::memories_read::

    { SelfSubject, About(&'a [String]) }` (`'self'` → `(aboutCharacterId = ?
    OR aboutCharacterId IS NULL)`; `About` dedups, drops the holder's own id,

    AND keystone = 1 AND ${HOT_TIER_SQL} AND ${subjectSql} ORDER BY createdAt
    ASC, id ASC`; rows hydrated with context `'keystone fetch'`);
    `count_keystones(conn, character_id, about_character_id: Option<&str>,
    exclude_id: Option<&str>) -> Result<i64, DbError>` (`self = !about ||

    `consolidatedAt` and `createdAt`; `hydrate_raw_rows` coerces number /
    bigint / null, drops a string cell with the WARN; `zod_memory_issues`

    argument (`PolicyProtection::Keystone` FIRST; `SparedBy { keystone,
    manual, digest, cold }` in that key order). `api/types.rs` `MemoryList.
    keystone: Option<bool>` (query `keystone=true`); the create / update
    request twins accept `keystone: z.boolean().optional()`; the three API
    arms carry v4's bytes (`A character may keep at most 5 keystones about
    one subject; unmark one first.`, `An archived memory cannot be a
    keystone; it belongs to the digest that replaced it.`, `The memory was

    could not be marked a keystone.`).
  - **C-4 `RoleplayTemplateColumns-v2` (consumed by P4.D351):** in `db/

    match_curly_quotes: Option<bool>, #[serde(rename="closeOptional", …)]
    close_optional: Option<bool> }`; `#[derive(Serialize, Deserialize)]
    #[serde(rename_all="camelCase")] pub struct UnmarkedStyle { pub style:

    add_ons: Option<DelimiterAddOns> }`; `RtCreate { …, narration_delimiters:
    Option<StringOrPair> /* None = unmarked prose */, unmarked_style:
    Option<UnmarkedStyle>, markdown_asterisks: bool }`; `RtUpdate { …,
    narration_delimiters: Option<Option<StringOrPair>> /* Some(None) = set
    null */, unmarked_style: Option<Option<UnmarkedStyle>>, markdown_
    asterisks: Option<bool> }`; `pub fn encode_narration_delimiters(v:
    Option<&StringOrPair>) -> String` (JSON text — `'*'` → `"*"`, a pair →
    `["[","]"]`, `None` → the text `null`); `pub fn decode_narration_
    delimiters(stored: Option<&str>) -> Option<StringOrPair>` (SQL NULL →
    `None`; a `[string,string]` → itself; non-string / empty → `'*'`;
    JSON-parsable → null / string / pair; non-JSON text → the RAW legacy
    string); `marshal_rt_row` emits `narrationDelimiters` DECODED (JSON null
    when `None`), `unmarkedStyle` OMITTED on SQL NULL, `markdownAsterisks`
    Bool (NULL cell → `true`); `RT_COLUMNS` gains the two columns LAST;
    `SeedUpdate { …, narration_delimiters: String /* ENCODED */, unmarked_
    style_json: Option<String>, markdown_asterisks: bool }`; `RenderingPattern.
    scope` accepts `unmarked | literal | strip` with an empty `className`.
    The keystone regenerates NO patterns; the Novel row's are the dump's
    bytes. P4.D351 owns `api/roleplay_templates.rs`, `services/annotations.
    rs`, `template_prompt_hint.rs` and the four carriers' rows.
  - **C8 — the keystone memory wire (consumed by P4.D359):** `memoryList`

- **C9 — the WCAG rules R1–R12 (P4.D358 writes; EVERY SPA lane applies to
  the files it owns and records a HANDOFF for the census):**
  - **R1 Modal dialog surface** (`dff76ebef`): the dialog CARD (never the
    overlay) carries `role="dialog"` — `role="alertdialog"` where the
    dialog-roles table says so — `aria-modal="true"`, `aria-labelledby=
    "<titleId>"` with `id="<titleId>"` on the visible title element (a
    per-instance id: `inject(UniqueId)` if the SPA has one, else a module
    counter `qt-dialog-<n>`); `tabindex="-1"`; the shared focus directive
    (`qtDialogFocus`, P4.D358's `ui/dialog-focus.ts`) for focus-in / Tab-trap
    / restore; Escape closes unless the host's busy gate says otherwise (keep
    v4's gate expression). For a `qt-modal` host: nothing — the primitive
    does it.
  - **R2 Non-modal floating surface** (`FloatingDialog`): `role="dialog"
    aria-modal="false" aria-labelledby`, `tabindex="-1"`, focus in on open
    and back to the opener on close, NO Tab trap, close button `aria-label=
    "Close"`, Escape inside closes it (topmost: `stopPropagation`), Escape
    elsewhere closes it only while `!document.querySelector('[aria-modal=
    "true"]')`, both skipping `defaultPrevented`.
  - **R3 Icon-only buttons** carry v4's `aria-label` byte: `Close`, `Cancel`,
    `Dismiss message`, `Close gallery`, `` Remove alias ${alias} ``, `Up one
    folder`, `Toggle model list` (+ `aria-expanded`).
  - **R4 Visible label ↔ control:** `<label for="<id>">` + `id="<id>"` on the
    control; never a bare `<span>` caption beside a control.
  - **R5 Heading-captioned control:** `aria-labelledby="<headingId>"` with
    `id` on the heading (ProfilesTab / ImageGenerationCard / ModelBehaviorCard
    / FieldSelectionStep / SuggestionCard / ContextCompression switch).
  - **R6 Placeholder-only / caption-less control:** `aria-label` with v4's
    string (never invent a string; where v4 has none, do not add one).
  - **R7 Switch:** `role="switch"` + `aria-checked` + a name (`aria-label` or
    `aria-labelledby`); the Prospero one is `type="button"` too.
  - **R8 Clickable non-button:** `role="button" tabindex="0" (keydown)=
    "activateOnKey($event)"` (P4.D358's `ui/activate-on-key.ts`) with
    `aria-expanded` (FileTable) / `aria-pressed` (gallery tile, quick-hide
    tags) where v4 sets them. Prefer a real `<button>` where the layout
    allows (v4 kept spans only inside `<tr>` / heading contexts and under
    the uppercase-button themes).
  - **R9 Live region for notices:** the toast container `role="status"
    aria-live="polite" data-toast-container`; an error toast `role="alert"`
    (P4.D358 only).
  - **R10 Colour-only state gets words:** `<span class="sr-only">Unmoderated
    chat.</span>`; ` (overheard)` appended to the whisper label.
  - **R11 Async result announced:** `<span class="sr-only" role="status"
    aria-live="polite">…</span>` (DocumentPane: `editingIndicator ??
    (saveLabel === 'Saved' ? 'Saved' : '')`).
  - **R12 Pointer targets ≥ 24 px** — CSS, P4.D357 (`.qt-tab-close`);
    templates: `min-w-6 min-h-6` on the inline copy-id button (P4.D358).
  The `e2e/support/toasts.ts` helper: `export const toastStack = (page:
